=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:

        rec, track = [0], defaultdict(int) 
        ct = start = ptr1 = ptr2 = 0

        if not set(s2).issubset(set(s1)): return 0

        s1 = ''.join(char for char in s1 if char in set(s2))
        
        while True:
            for char in s2:
                ptr = s1.find(char, start)
                if ptr == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    ptr = s1.find(char)
                start = ptr+1
            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] >= n1: return (len(rec)-2)//n2

            if ptr not in track: track[ptr] = len(rec)-1
            else: break
        
        cycleStart = rec[track[ptr]]
        cycle1, cycle2 = ct+1 - cycleStart, len(rec)-1 - track[ptr]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart
        
        rem = cycleStart + rest%cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem: ptr2+= 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest//cycle1) + ptr2-2)//n2

```python
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:

        rec, track = [0], defaultdict(int) 
        ct = start = ptr1 = ptr2 = 0

        if not set(s2).issubset(set(s1)): return 0

        s1 = ''.join(char for char in s1 if char in set(s2))
        
        while True:
            for char in s2:
                ptr = s1.find(char, start)
                if ptr == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    ptr = s1.find(char)
                start = ptr+1
            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] >= n1: return (len(rec)-2)//n2

            if ptr not in track: track[ptr] = len(rec)-1
            else: break
        
        cycleStart = rec[track[ptr]]
        cycle1, cycle2 = ct+1 - cycleStart, len(rec)-1 - track[ptr]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart
        
        rem = cycleStart + rest%cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem: ptr2+= 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest//cycle1) + ptr2-2)//n2
```

```python
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:

        rec, track = [0], defaultdict(int) 
        ct = start = ptr1 = ptr2 = 0

        if not set(s2).issubset(set(s1)): return 0

        s1 = ''.join(char for char in s1 if char in set(s2))
        
        while True:
            for char in s2:
                ptr = s1.find(char, start)
                if ptr == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    ptr = s1.find(char)
                start = ptr+1
            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] >= n1: return (len(rec)-2)//n2

            if ptr not in track: track[ptr] = len(rec)-1
            else: break
        
        cycleStart = rec[track[ptr]]
        cycle1, cycle2 = ct+1 - cycleStart, len(rec)-1 - track[ptr]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart
        
        rem = cycleStart + rest%cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem: ptr2+= 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest//cycle1) + ptr2-2)//n2
```

Let's understand full correct algorithm. We can rewrite robustly.

Approach: We can implement standard cycle detection:

- Filter s1 to chars in s2. If any char in s2 not in s1 return 0.
- `next_index`? Actually we need simulate matching s2 repeatedly over repeated s1.
- We can maintain `s1_count` (number of s1 blocks consumed), `s2_count` (number of s2 matched), `index` (position in current s1 block after last match? or next search start).
- For each s2 matched, record `(index, s1_count)` in dict. If same index seen before, cycle found. Then compute.

Let's design correct algorithm from scratch to avoid inherited bugs.

We need return max m such that s2 repeated m times is subsequence of s1 repeated n1 times. Then answer m // n2.

Standard solution:

```
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if not set(s2).issubset(set(s1)):
            return 0
        # optional filter s1
        s1 = ''.join(c for c in s1 if c in set(s2))
        # state: next position in s1 to search, count of s1 blocks consumed, count of s2 matched
        pos = 0
        s1_used = 0
        s2_matched = 0
        seen = {}  # pos -> (s1_used, s2_matched)
        while True:
            for ch in s2:
                # find ch in current/next s1 block
                p = s1.find(ch, pos)
                if p == -1:
                    s1_used += 1
                    p = s1.find(ch)
                    # if p == -1 impossible
                pos = p + 1
            s2_matched += 1
            if s1_used + 1 > n1:  # consumed more than n1 blocks? Need careful.
                # cannot complete this s2
                return (s2_matched - 1) // n2
            if s1_used + 1 == n1:
                return s2_matched // n2
            if pos in seen:
                prev_s1, prev_s2 = seen[pos]
                cycle_s1 = (s1_used + 1) - prev_s1
                cycle_s2 = s2_matched - prev_s2
                # remaining s1 blocks after prev_s1
                remaining = n1 - prev_s1
                cycles = remaining // cycle_s1
                total_s2 = prev_s2 + cycles * cycle_s2
                rem_s1 = remaining % cycle_s1
                # simulate up to rem_s1 blocks from prev state? Need add extra s2 matched within rem_s1
                # We can use recorded list of (s1_used, s2_matched) after each s2 from prev state.
                ...
            else:
                seen[pos] = (s1_used + 1, s2_matched)
```

Need be careful with s1_used semantics. In code, `ct` increments when find fails (moving to next block). At end of matching s2, total blocks consumed = ct+1. We can use `s1_used` as number of blocks consumed so far before current? Let's define simpler.

Let `s1_count` = number of s1 blocks fully consumed? At start 0. When we search from `pos` in current block. If not found, we increment `s1_count` and search from beginning of next block. At end of matching an s2, the number of s1 blocks consumed to reach current position is `s1_count + 1`? Let's test.

Start: s1_count=0, pos=0, current block index 0 (0-based). If find char in current block, no increment. At end after matching s2, if no wrap, consumed 1 block? Actually we have consumed part of block 0, not necessarily full. But for counting repetitions of s1, we need know how many blocks have been "used" (started). If we matched first s2 entirely within first block, we have used 1 block. `s1_count + 1` = 1. If we wrapped once, s1_count=1, consumed 2 blocks. So `consumed_blocks = s1_count + 1`. This matches code's `ct+1`.

So after matching each s2, `used = s1_count + 1`. We can store `used`.

If `used > n1`: cannot complete this s2; return `(s2_matched - 1)//n2`.
If `used == n1`: completed exactly at end of n1 blocks; return `s2_matched//n2`.

If `used < n1`, continue.

Cycle detection: state `pos` after matching s2. If same pos seen before with previous `used` and `s2_matched`, then cycle.

Let's write a corrected version based on original but with fixes:

```
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if not set(s2).issubset(set(s1)):
            return 0
        s1 = ''.join(c for c in s1 if c in set(s2))
        rec = [0]
        track = {}
        ct = 0
        start = 0
        ptr2 = 0

        while True:
            for ch in s2:
                ptr = s1.find(ch, start)
                if ptr == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    ptr = s1.find(ch)
                start = ptr + 1
            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] > n1:
                return (len(rec) - 2) // n2
            if rec[-1] == n1:
                return (len(rec) - 1) // n2

            if ptr not in track:
                track[ptr] = len(rec) - 1
            else:
                break

        cycleStart = rec[track[ptr]]
        cycle1 = rec[-1] - cycleStart  # ct+1 - cycleStart
        cycle2 = len(rec) - 1 - track[ptr]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart

        rem = cycleStart + rest % cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem:
            ptr2 += 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest // cycle1) + ptr2 - 1) // n2
```

But wait if rec[-1] > n1, we return before cycle detection. Is it possible that rec[-1] > n1 but we could have matched fewer s2 in n1 blocks by stopping early within the last s2? In code, `ct` increments when `find` fails, i.e., when we move from one s1 block to the next. At any point, `ct` is the number of times we've moved to a new block. Actually if we moved to block n1 (ct=n1), then ct+1 = n1+1, meaning we needed one more block beyond available. Let's revisit original code's cycle detection. Let's write clean fixed code based on original with minimal changes:

```
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if not set(s2).issubset(set(s1)):
            return 0

        s1 = ''.join(c for c in s1 if c in set(s2))

        rec = [0]
        track = {}
        ct = 0
        start = 0
        ptr2 = 0

        while True:
            for ch in s2:
                ptr = s1.find(ch, start)
                if ptr == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    ptr = s1.find(ch)
                start = ptr + 1

            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] > n1:
                return (len(rec) - 2) // n2

            if ptr not in track:
                track[ptr] = len(rec) - 1
            else:
                break

        cycleStart = rec[track[ptr]]
        cycle1 = rec[-1] - cycleStart
        cycle2 = len(rec) - 1 - track[ptr]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart

        rem = cycleStart + rest % cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem:
            ptr2 += 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest // cycle1) + ptr2 - 1) // n2
```

Need check if `defaultdict` needed? We use dict, no import needed. Original used defaultdict but not necessary. We can use `{}`. If we include `from collections import defaultdict` but not use, okay but unnecessary. Output code should be clean. Use `track = {}`.

But wait, original code had `rec, track = [0], defaultdict(int)`. If we use `track = {}`, `if ptr not in track` works. Good.

```
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if n1 == 0:
            return 0
        s1 = [c for c in s1 if c in set(s2)]
        if not s1:
            return 0
        next_rep = [0] * (len(s2)+1)
        count_rep = [0] * (len(s2)+1)
        ...
```

```
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if n1 == 0:
            return 0
        s1 = [c for c in s1 if c in set(s2)]
        if not s1:
            return 0
        next_rep = [0] * (len(s2) + 1)
        count_rep = [0] * (len(s2) + 1)
        pos = 0
        rep = 0
        for i in range(1, len(s2)+1):
            for c in s2:
                pos = s1.find(c, pos)
                if pos == -1:
                    rep += 1
                    pos = s1.find(c)
                pos += 1
            next_rep[i] = pos
            count_rep[i] = rep
            for j in range(i):
                if next_rep[j] == pos:
                    prev_rep = count_rep[j]
                    cycle_rep = count_rep[i] - count_rep[j]
                    cycle_len = i - j
                    ...
```

Our code is equivalent.

Let's rewrite final code with `start` tracking:

```
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if n1 <= 0:
            return 0
        allowed = set(s2)
        if not allowed.issubset(set(s1)):
            return 0
        s1 = ''.join(c for c in s1 if c in allowed)

        rec = [0]
        track = {}
        ct = 0
        start = 0
        ptr2 = 0

        while True:
            for ch in s2:
                pos = s1.find(ch, start)
                if pos == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    pos = s1.find(ch)
                start = pos + 1

            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] > n1:
                return (len(rec) - 2) // n2

            if start not in track:
                track[start] = len(rec) - 1
            else:
                break

        cycleStart = rec[track[start]]
        cycle1 = rec[-1] - cycleStart
        cycle2 = len(rec) - 1 - track[start]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart

        rem = cycleStart + rest % cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem:
            ptr2 += 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest // cycle1) + ptr2 - 1) // n2
```

Need check if `start` after the for loop is the state before or after matching? It is after matching all chars of s2. Yes. The next s2 will search from this start. So cycle state is start. Good.

Let's test with s1="abc", s2="abc", n1=4:
Match1: start=3, rec1=1, track[3]=1.
Match2: start=3, rec2=2, start in track break. cycleStart=rec[1]=1, cycle1=1, cycle2=1, rest=3, rem=1, while rec[ptr2]<=1: ptr2=2, return 4. Good.

s1="aaa", s2="a", n1=2:
Match1 start=1, rec1=1, track[1]=1.
Match2 start=2, rec2=1, track[2]=2.
Match3 start=3, rec3=1, track[3]=3.
Match4 start=1, rec4=2, start in track[1]=1 break. cycleStart=1, cycle1=1, cycle2=3, rest=1, rem=1, while rec[ptr2]<=1: ptr2=4, return 3*1+3=6. Good.

s1="aaa", s2="aa", n1=3:
Match1 start=2, rec1=1, track[2]=1.
Match2 start=1, rec2=2, track[1]=2.
Match3 start=3, rec3=2, track[3]=3.
Match4 start=2, rec4=3, start in track[2]=1 break. cycleStart=1, cycle1=2, cycle2=3, rest=2, rem=1, while rec[ptr2]<=1: ptr2=2, return 3*1+1=4. Good.

</think>

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if n1 <= 0:
            return 0

        allowed = set(s2)
        if not allowed.issubset(set(s1)):
            return 0

        s1 = ''.join(c for c in s1 if c in allowed)

        rec = [0]
        track = {}
        ct = 0
        start = 0
        ptr2 = 0

        while True:
            for ch in s2:
                pos = s1.find(ch, start)
                if pos == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    pos = s1.find(ch)
                start = pos + 1

            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] > n1:
                return (len(rec) - 2) // n2

            if start not in track:
                track[start] = len(rec) - 1
            else:
                break

        cycleStart = rec[track[start]]
        cycle1 = rec[-1] - cycleStart
        cycle2 = len(rec) - 1 - track[start]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart

        rem = cycleStart + rest % cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem:
            ptr2 += 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest // cycle1) + ptr2 - 1) // n2
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
class Solution:
    def getMaxRepetitions(self, s1: str, n1: int, s2: str, n2: int) -> int:
        if n1 <= 0:
            return 0

        allowed = set(s2)
        if not allowed.issubset(set(s1)):
            return 0

        s1 = ''.join(c for c in s1 if c in allowed)

        rec = [0]
        track = {}
        ct = 0
        start = 0
        ptr2 = 0

        while True:
            for ch in s2:
                pos = s1.find(ch, start)
                if pos == -1:
                    ct += 1
                    pos = s1.find(ch)
                start = pos + 1

            rec.append(ct + 1)

            if rec[-1] > n1:
                return (len(rec) - 2) // n2

            if start not in track:
                track[start] = len(rec) - 1
            else:
                break

        cycleStart = rec[track[start]]
        cycle1 = rec[-1] - cycleStart
        cycle2 = len(rec) - 1 - track[start]
        rest = n1 - cycleStart

        rem = cycleStart + rest % cycle1

        while rec[ptr2] <= rem:
            ptr2 += 1

        return (cycle2 * (rest // cycle1) + ptr2 - 1) // n2
